November 1975.

December 1975 both days inclusive vice Shri P. S. 
Vaidya granted leave.

THE INSTITUTE OF CHARTERED ACCOUNTANTS 
OF INDIA 


Indore, the 22nd November 1975 

No. 1/1975. — In pursuance of Regulations 55(1) and 
56 of the Subsidiary Banks General Regulations, 1959, 
the Board of Dircctois of the State Bank of Indore has 
empowered the Law Officer to exercise the powers speci- 
fied hereunder : — 

(a) To endorse and transfer promissory potes. 
stock receipts, stock debentures, shares, securi- 
ties and documents of title to goods, standing 
in the name of or held by the State Bank of 
Indore, to draw, accept and endorse bills of 
exchange and cheques, to issue, confirm and 
transfer letters of credit, to sign guarantees and 
indemnities, in the current and authorised busi- 
ness of I he State Bank of Indore and to sign 
all other letters, advices, accounts receipts and 
documents connected with such business or 
other current or authorised business of the State 
Bank of Indore. 

(b) To sign and verify plaints, written statements, 
petitions and applications, to swear and affrm 
affidavits, to sign, seal and deliver bonds and 
generally to make and complete all other docu- 
ments connected with legal proceedings on be- 
half of the State Bank of Indore. 

B. K. MOOKERJEA, 
Managing Director 


Indore, the 1st December 1975 
NOTICE 

NOTICE IS hereby given that the Register of Share- 
holders of the State Bank of Indore will be closed for 
transfer of shares from Tuesday the 27th January 1976 
to Tuesday the 10th February, 1976 both days inclusive. 

By order of the Board of Directors. 

B, K. MOOKERJEA, 
Managing Director
